Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The riemann_surfaces variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 450 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 921 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to riemann_surfaces theory, we decompose the problem into 450 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
